    <script type="text/javascript">
        const getLoginState = () => new Promise((resolve) => {
            const sessionLoginState = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('LOGIN_STATE') || null);
            
            if(sessionLoginState) resolve(sessionLoginState);
           
            document.addEventListener('LOGIN_FINISHED', (e) => resolve(e?.detail || null), { once: true });
        });
        
        async function hasBannerFreeSubscription() {
            let loginState = await getLoginState();
            
            if(loginState === null || !loginState?.loggedIn) return false;
            
            let endpoint = "/api/getUserAdsEnablement";
            const url = [endpoint, "?", loginState?.hashedEmail || ''].join('');
           
            return fetch(url)
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(({ result }) => !result);
        }
        
        async function handleBannerFreeSubscription() {
            return hasBannerFreeSubscription()
                .then((result) => {
                    if (result === false) return;
                    
                    window.enable_banner_ads = false;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.adblock,.adform__text,.grid-ad,.adform__topbanner,.finddet-widget,.section-sticky-banners,.adform__sticky').forEach(b => b.remove());
                }
            );
        }

        async function setEnabledAdPositions() {
            let loginState = await getLoginState();
            
            if(loginState === null || !loginState?.loggedIn) return;
            
           if(Array.isArray(undefined)) {
                // Restrict which ad positions are enabled, when user is logged in
                window.enabledAdPositions = undefined;
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="application/javascript">
        const currentUrl = new URL(window.location.href);
        const autologinReferral = currentUrl.searchParams.get('autologin-referral');

        if (autologinReferral || autologinReferral === '') {
            window.jfmReferrer = decodeURIComponent(autologinReferral);
            
            /* 
            Remove the autologin-referral query parameter from the URL in the address bar without touching 
            the history, in order to:
               1. Avoid any existing autologin-referral being passed on as part of the return URL on refresh 
               (ref-ception)
               2. Make the URL look pretty in the address bar 
            */
            currentUrl.searchParams.delete('autologin-referral');
            window.history.replaceState({}, document.title, currentUrl.toString());
            
            // Overwrite document.referrer with the autologin-referral
            Object.defineProperty(window.document, 'referrer', {
                writable: true,
                value: window.jfmReferrer
            });
        } else {
           window.jfmReferrer = document.referrer;
        }
    </script>    <script type="application/javascript">
        (function() {
            // In Pugpig app context, authentication is handled by PugpigAutoLogin.
            // Skip SSO autologin entirely and don't signal redirectAttempted yet - 
            // PugpigAutoLogin may reload the page after syncing authentication state.
            if (window.isPugpigContext) {
                console.info("Autologin - Skipping (Pugpig context)");
                return;
            }
            
            const hasAidJwtCookie = document.cookie.split(';').some(function (cookie) {
                return cookie.trim().indexOf('aid.jwt=') === 0;
            });
            
            const isRedirectedFromSsoServer = document.referrer && document.referrer.includes('https://jfmid.dk');
            const isRedirectedFromSsoServerLogout = document.referrer && document.referrer.includes('https://jfmid.dk/log-ud?return_url=');
            const isRedirectedFromSsoClient = autologinReferral !== null;
            
            // Do not redirect if already redirected from SSO server or client (unless it was a logout),
            // or if the aid.jwt cookie is already set (the user is authenticated with aID means aID controls autologin).
            if (isRedirectedFromSsoServer 
                && !isRedirectedFromSsoServerLogout
                || isRedirectedFromSsoClient
                || hasAidJwtCookie) {
                console.info("Autologin - Skipping");
                window.redirectAttempted = true;
            } else {
                window.redirectAttempted = false;
                console.info("Autologin - Checking");
                const sessionLoginStateSerialized = sessionStorage.getItem('LOGIN_STATE');
                const sessionLoginState = sessionLoginStateSerialized ? JSON.parse(sessionLoginStateSerialized) : null;
                
                const currentUrl = new URL(window.location.href);
                
                // Add autologin-referral query parameter to the return_url
                if(document.referrer || document.referrer === ''){
                    currentUrl.searchParams.set('autologin-referral', encodeURIComponent(document.referrer));
                }
                
                window.redirectAttempted = ssoClientRedirect(sessionLoginState ? sessionLoginState.loggedIn : false, currentUrl.toString());
            }
            if (window.redirectAttempted) {
                const event = new Event('sso_redirect_attempted');
                document.dispatchEvent(event);
            }
        })();
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    function findAdContainers(position, callback) {
        const positionName = position.dataset.adPositionName;
    
        // Select ad containers based on the provided position name
        let adContainers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-container="' + positionName + '"]');
    
        // If no ad containers are found, try to find the closest ad container from a given position element
        if (adContainers.length === 0) {
            const closestAdContainer = position.closest('[data-ad-container]');
            adContainers = closestAdContainer ? [closestAdContainer] : [];
        }
    
        if (adContainers.length === 0) return;
    
        adContainers.forEach(callback);
    }
    
    async function checkEnabledAdPositions() {        
        if (!window.enabledAdPositions) return;
    
        const adPositions = document.querySelectorAll('[data-ad-position-name]');
        
        adPositions.forEach((position) => {
            const positionName = position.getAttribute('data-ad-position-name');
    
            const isEnabledPosition = window.enabledAdPositions?.includes(positionName);
    
            if (! isEnabledPosition) {
                findAdContainers(position, (adContainer) => {
                    adContainer.style.display = 'none';
                });
            }
        });
    }
</script>

    <script>
    (function () {
        var storageKey = 'nordjyske-theme-preference';
        var blackWeekStorageKey = 'nordjyske-theme-preference-blackweek-2026';
        var preferences = ['system', 'light', 'dark'];
        var darkModeQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');
        var appliedStorageKey;

        function isPreference(value) {
            return preferences.indexOf(value) !== -1;
        }

        function isBlackWeek() {
            var now = Date.now();
            return now >= Date.parse('2026-11-23T00:00:00+01:00')
                && now < Date.parse('2026-11-30T00:00:00+01:00');
        }

        function activeStorageKey() {
            return isBlackWeek() ? blackWeekStorageKey : storageKey;
        }

        function readPreference() {
            var defaultPreference = isBlackWeek() ? 'dark' : 'system';
            try {
                var savedPreference = localStorage.getItem(activeStorageKey());
                return isPreference(savedPreference) ? savedPreference : defaultPreference;
            } catch {
                return defaultPreference;
            }
        }

        function resolveTheme(preference) {
            return preference === 'system'
                ? (darkModeQuery.matches ? 'dark' : 'light')
                : preference;
        }

        function updateControls(preference) {
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-theme-preference-value]').forEach(function (control) {
                control.checked = control.value === preference;
            });
        }

        function applyPreference(preference, persist) {
            appliedStorageKey = activeStorageKey();
            if (!isPreference(preference)) {
                preference = 'system';
            }

            var resolvedTheme = resolveTheme(preference);
            var root = document.documentElement;
            var previousTheme = root.dataset.theme;
            root.dataset.theme = resolvedTheme;
            root.dataset.themePreference = preference;
            root.dataset.bsTheme = resolvedTheme;
            updateControls(preference);

            window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
            if (previousTheme && previousTheme !== resolvedTheme) {
                window.dataLayer.push({
                    event: 'theme_change',
                    theme: resolvedTheme
                });
            } else {
                window.dataLayer.push({ theme: resolvedTheme });
            }

            if (persist) {
                try {
                    localStorage.setItem(activeStorageKey(), preference);
                } catch {
                    // The selected theme still applies for the current page.
                }
            }

            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('nordjyske:themechange', {
                detail: { preference: preference, theme: resolvedTheme }
            }));
        }

        document.addEventListener('change', function (event) {
            var control = event.target.closest('[data-theme-preference-value]');
            if (control) {
                applyPreference(control.value, true);
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            updateControls(readPreference());
        });

        function handleSystemThemeChange() {
            var preference = readPreference();
            if (preference === 'system') {
                applyPreference(preference, false);
            }
        }

        if (typeof darkModeQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {
            darkModeQuery.addEventListener('change', handleSystemThemeChange);
        } else {
            darkModeQuery.addListener(handleSystemThemeChange);
        }

        window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {
            if (event.key === activeStorageKey()) {
                applyPreference(readPreference(), false);
            }
        });

        window.addEventListener('focus', function () {
            if (appliedStorageKey !== activeStorageKey()) {
                applyPreference(readPreference(), false);
            }
        });

        applyPreference(readPreference(), false);
    }());
</script>

<div class="container position-relative py-gutter">
    <div ad="" ad-prefix="nordjyske" ad-type-desktop="header-wide" ad-margin="b" ad-intro="Annonce" class="ad-unit ad-unit--header-wide"></div>
    
    <article data-story-article data-id="f7756f5e-9204-42e9-9425-79808f573cb2"
             data-articleid="6251378"
             data-url="https://nordjyske.dk/nyheder/politik/minister-vil-beskytte-foelsomme-data-mod-kvantecomputere/6251378"
             data-primarytag="Politik"
             data-categories="[&quot;Politik&quot;,&quot;Emne&quot;,&quot;Nyheder&quot;]"

             data-audio-requires-access="true"

             data-is-ritzau="True"

             itemscope
             itemtype="http://schema.org/NewsArticle">
        <div id="article-start" style="height: 1px;"></div>
            <h1 class="display-1 mb-4" itemprop="headline" headline-text="">Minister vil beskytte f&#xF8;lsomme data mod kvantecomputere</h1>
            <p class="fs-3 mb-4" itemprop="abstract">Ny hjemmeside skal hj&#xE6;lpe virksomheder og myndigheder med at beskytte data mod fremtidens computere.</p>
        <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-3 mb-4">

            <a href="/tag/politik" class="article-tag d-inline-block text-primary text-uppercase" itemprop="articleSection">
                Politik
            </a>
        </div>
        <div class="mb-4">
        <div class="d-flex flex-column gap-2">
            <picture>
                <source media="(min-width:1280px)" srcset="https://image.nordjyske.dk/users/nordjyske/images/167274521.jpg?t[strip]=true&amp;t[quality]=85&amp;t[crop][width]=4000&amp;t[crop][height]=2250&amp;t[crop][x]=0&amp;t[crop][y]=0&amp;t[resize][width]=1280&amp;t[resize][height]=720&amp;accessToken=ea9b2360d5240cd3619f3907de324f02b40f1bd90fc8b96865ae0e77f2fd16f8" width="1280" height="720">
                <source media="(min-width:1272px)" srcset="https://image.nordjyske.dk/users/nordjyske/images/167274521.jpg?t[strip]=true&amp;t[quality]=85&amp;t[crop][width]=4000&amp;t[crop][height]=2250&amp;t[crop][x]=0&amp;t[crop][y]=0&amp;t[resize][width]=1216&amp;t[resize][height]=684&amp;accessToken=2039dee27640f33cc8bd71c2100c10f5a225df5022312e8df8657d154052fffa" width="1216" height="684">
                <source media="(min-width:1024px)" srcset="https://image.nordjyske.dk/users/nordjyske/images/167274521.jpg?t[strip]=true&amp;t[quality]=85&amp;t[crop][width]=4000&amp;t[crop][height]=2250&amp;t[crop][x]=0&amp;t[crop][y]=0&amp;t[resize][width]=976&amp;t[resize][height]=549&amp;accessToken=fcc4ce95fd284b2140a8b857c600bf1b4737a12d487ab4e27e6c848a3068680e" width="976" height="549">
                <img src="https://image.nordjyske.dk/users/nordjyske/images/167274521.jpg?t[strip]=true&amp;t[quality]=85&amp;t[crop][width]=4000&amp;t[crop][height]=2248&amp;t[crop][x]=0&amp;t[crop][y]=0&amp;t[resize][width]=984&amp;t[resize][height]=553&amp;accessToken=85bf7687cc40e72681a34f527567cac181f7822147457f18c126d433e1be121a"
                     alt="Det er kun et sp&#xF8;rgsm&#xE5;l om tid, f&#xF8;r kvantecomputere er s&#xE5; kraftfulde, at de kan bryde igennem de sikkerhedsforanstaltninger, der i dag beskytter f&#xF8;lsomme data, siger minister for samfundssikkerhed og beredskab Lisbeth Bech-Nielsen. (Arkivfoto)."
                     width="984"
                     height="553"
                     style="width: 100%; height: auto;"
                     loading="eager"
                     fetchpriority="high">
            </picture>
                <div class="fs-5 text-body-tertiary">
                    Det er kun et spørgsmål om tid, før kvantecomputere er så kraftfulde, at de kan bryde igennem de sikkerhedsforanstaltninger, der i dag beskytter følsomme data, siger minister for samfundssikkerhed og beredskab Lisbeth Bech-Nielsen. (Arkivfoto). <i>Tom Little/Reuters</i>
                </div>
        </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="mb-gutter border-top border-bottom py-4 px-md-5 d-flex flex-wrap flex-md-nowrap column-gap-6 row-gap-4 justify-content-between">
                    <div class="d-flex flex-column align-self-center column-gap-6 row-gap-4">
                                <div class="byline position-relative" itemprop="author" itemscope itemtype="https://schema.org/Person">
                                    <div class="byline__portrait">
RP                                    </div>
                                    <div class="byline__body">
                                        <div class="byline__name">
                                                <span itemprop="name">Ritzau Politik</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                    </div>
                <div class="fs-5 align-self-center flex-shrink-0 text-md-end">
                    <span itemprop="datePublished">17. november 2026 kl. 06:00</span>
                </div>
        </div>
        <div class="row gy-gutter">
            <div class="col-12 col-lg-8 order-1 order-lg-0">
                <articlebody itemprop="articleBody">
                    <div class="d-flex flex-column mb-cr gap-5 mb-5 non-paywall">
                            
            
<p>I fremtiden vil kvantecomputere kunne bryde de sikkerhedsforanstaltninger, der i dag beskytter følsomme data.</p>

            
<p>Derfor er det vigtigt, at myndigheder og virksomheder i de kommende år omstiller sig til at kunne afværge truslen fra kvantecomputere, mener Lisbeth Bech-Nielsen (SF), der er minister for samfundssikkerhed og beredskab.</p>

            
<p>Til det formål har Styrelsen for Samfundssikkerhed i samarbejde med Forsvarets Efterretningstjeneste (FE) lanceret hjemmesiden kvantesikker.dk og to nye vejledninger, skriver ministeriet i en pressemeddelelse tirsdag.</p>

                    </div>
                        
                            <div id="poool-widget">
                                <!-- Poool paywall will be displayed here -->

    <script type="text/javascript">
    window.playableGrantConsent = function () {
        this.setupPlayableScript(true);
        this.pushPlayableConsentAndTrackingData();
    }

    window.playableRevokeConsent = function () {
        this.setupPlayableScript(false);
    }

    function setupPlayableScript(consentGranted) {
        const playableScriptId = 'playableScript';
        const playableScript = document.getElementById(playableScriptId);

        if (consentGranted === true) {
            if (!playableScript) {
                const playableScriptTag = document.createElement('script');
                playableScriptTag.id = playableScriptId;
                playableScriptTag.type = 'text/javascript';
                playableScriptTag.async = true;
                playableScriptTag.src = 'https://popup.campaign.playable.com/script/c0dbfa42101110083a472a97715598dd/popup.min.js';
                document.head.appendChild(playableScriptTag);
            }
        }
        else if (playableScript) {
            playableScript.remove();
        }
    }

    function pushPlayableConsentAndTrackingData() {
        let loginState = getLoginState();
        let subscriptionState = getSubscriptionState();
        let subscribedNewsletters = getSubscribedNewsletters();
        let birthdayState = getBirthdayState();
        let tags = getTags();

        window.dataLayer.push({ 
            event: 'PlayableConsent', 
            loginState: loginState,
            birthdayState: birthdayState,
            subscriptionState: subscriptionState,
            newsletters: subscribedNewsletters,
            tags: tags
        });

        function getLoginState() {
            if (window.currentUser.IsLoggedIn)
                return 'logged-in';
            else if (document.cookie.split(';').find(row => row.trimStart().startsWith('PreviouslyLoggedIn')))
                return 'logged-out';
            else
                return 'unknown-user';
        };

        function getSubscriptionState() {
            if (!Array.isArray(window.currentUser.Subscriptions) || !window.currentUser.Subscriptions.length)
                return 'subscribed-free';
            else if (window.currentUser.Subscriptions.includes('Digital'))
                return 'subscribed-premium';
            else if (window.currentUser.Subscriptions.includes('nordjyske.dk'))
                return 'subscribed-basic';
            else
                return 'subscribed-other';
        }

        function getSubscribedNewsletters() {
            if (!Array.isArray(window.currentUser.NewsletterInterests) || !window.currentUser.NewsletterInterests.length)
                return ['newsletter-unknown'];
            else
                return Array.from(window.currentUser.NewsletterInterests);
        }

        function getBirthdayState() {
            return window.currentUser.BirthdayIsKnown
                ? 'user-birthdate-known'
                : 'user-birthdate-unknown';
        }

        function getTags() {
            var tags = document.querySelectorAll('#article-tags a');

            if (!tags)
                return [];

            return Array.from(tags).map(function (tag) {
                return tag.dataset.name.toLowerCase();
            });
        };
    }
</script>
